Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a bio algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 159. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 602/3.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on bio structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 159. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 602. QED.

Theorem 213.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the bio structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 492. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 195 and converges at rate O(1/n^1).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 492 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the bio structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 195. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
